                            subtree_text: Toys for Bob, Inc. is an American video game developer based in Novato, California , and founded in 1989. The founders, Paul Reiche III and Fred Ford (both pictured) , met when Reiche sought a programmer to develop Star Control , and developed several games, including The Horde , Pandemonium! , and The Unholy War . In the early 2000s, the studio transitioned to working on licensed games before being laid off by Crystal Dynamics. It was incorporated in 2002 and Activision became their publisher b
